## Goal

Declare the Tasks extension to receive a task handle. Poll or cancel the task, or receive progress while waiting. Read skills and follow resource updates through the same endpoint. These are recorded examples. Changing measurements and progress counters use labelled placeholders. Stream read boundaries are combined; progress shows representative first and last samples. Repeated working-task polls are shown once per task. Complete recordings and checks remain in internal evidence.

Category: API calls. This page has 19 step(s). 19 passed.

## Before you start

- The mcp address is `https://localhost:8443/mcp`.
- A bearer access token with the scopes read, write, admin. The commands show `<YOUR_ACCESS_TOKEN>` where your token goes.

Endpoint: `https://localhost:8443/mcp`

```json
        "version": "1"
      },
      "io.modelcontextprotocol/clientCapabilities": {}
    }
  },
  "jsonrpc": "2.0",
  "id": 8
}
```

### MCP: Acknowledge a subscription before sending its resource update

Flag: **PASSED**

Where: POST /mcp

Action: Acknowledge a subscription before sending its resource update

Expected result: The response matches the checks below.

Checks:

- PASSED: The acknowledgement arrives before the resource update.
- PASSED: The response has no MCP session header.
- PASSED: The client read the event stream.

Endpoint: `https://localhost:8443/mcp`

Header: `authorization: Bearer <YOUR_ACCESS_TOKEN>`

JSON-RPC request:

```json
{
  "jsonrpc": "2.0",
  "id": 2,
  "method": "subscriptions/listen",
  "params": {
    "notifications": {
      "resourceSubscriptions": [
        "state://operations/op-00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000015"
      ]
    },
    "_meta": {
      "io.modelcontextprotocol/protocolVersion": "2026-07-28",
      "io.modelcontextprotocol/clientInfo": {
        "name": "proxy-proof",
        "version": "1"
      },
      "io.modelcontextprotocol/clientCapabilities": {
        "extensions": {
          "io.modelcontextprotocol/tasks": {}
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
```

Request headers:

```json
{
  "accept": "application/json, text/event-stream",
  "content-type": "application/json",
  "mcp-method": "subscriptions/listen",
  "mcp-name": "",
  "mcp-protocol-version": "2026-07-28"
}
```

Response (HTTP 200), an event stream:

```text
event: message
data: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/subscriptions/acknowledged","params":{"_meta":{"io.modelcontextprotocol/subscriptionId":2},"notifications":{"resourceSubscriptions":["state://operations/op-00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000015"]}}}

event: message
data: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/resources/updated","params":{"_meta":{"io.modelcontextprotocol/subscriptionId":2},"uri":"state://operations/op-00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000015"}}


```

## If something goes wrong

If a step fails, read the [access errors](/troubleshooting/errors-access/), the [input errors](/troubleshooting/errors-input/), the [state errors](/troubleshooting/errors-state/) and the [server errors](/troubleshooting/errors-server/).
